/**
   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.
   *
   * Used both for handling individual JSDoc comments and for handling
   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param node The JsDoc Comment node to parse.
   * @param irNode
   * @return A JsDocInfoParser. Will contain either fileoverview JsDoc, or
   *     normal JsDoc, or no JsDoc (if the method parses to the wrong level).
   */
Method code:
464 |   private JsDocInfoParser createJsDocInfoParser(Comment node, Node irNode) {
465 |     String comment = node.getValue();
466 |     int lineno = node.getLineno();
467 |     int position = node.getAbsolutePosition();
468 | 
469 |     // The JsDocInfoParser expects the comment without the initial '/**'.
470 |     int numOpeningChars = 3;
471 |     JsDocInfoParser jsdocParser =
472 |       new JsDocInfoParser(
473 |           new JsDocTokenStream(comment.substring(numOpeningChars),
474 |                                lineno,
475 |                                position2charno(position) + numOpeningChars),
476 |           node,
477 |           irNode,
478 |           config,
479 |           errorReporter);
480 |     jsdocParser.setFileLevelJsDocBuilder(fileLevelJsDocBuilder);
481 |     jsdocParser.setFileOverviewJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
482 |     jsdocParser.parse();
483 |     return jsdocParser;
484 |   }

===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 515-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
515 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
516 |     int type = n.getType();
517 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
518 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
519 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
520 |     }
521 | 
522 |     curNode = n;
523 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) {
524 |       return;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
528 |       traverseFunction(n, parent);
529 |     } else {
530 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
531 |         // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
532 |         // would no longer point to the true next
533 |         Node next = child.getNext();
534 |         traverseBranch(child, n);
535 |         child = next;
536 |       }
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     curNode = n;
540 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
541 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.JSDocInfoBuilder.build(Node)

Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a {@link JSDocInfo} object based on the populated information and
   * returns it. Once this method is called, the builder can be reused to build
   * another {@link JSDocInfo} object.
   *
   * @param associatedNode The source node containing the JSDoc.
   * @return a {@link JSDocInfo} object populated with the values given to this
   *     builder. If no value was populated, this method simply returns
   *     {@code null}
   */
Method code:
133 |   public JSDocInfo build(Node associatedNode) {
134 |     if (populated) {
135 |       JSDocInfo built = currentInfo;
136 |       built.setAssociatedNode(associatedNode);
137 |       populateDefaults(built);
138 |       populated = false;
139 |       currentInfo = new JSDocInfo(this.parseDocumentation);
140 |       return built;
141 |     } else {
142 |       return null;
143 |     }
144 |   }

===== M0043 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 544-577
Documentation:
/** Traverses a function. */
Method code:
544 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
545 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
546 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
547 | 
548 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
549 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
550 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
551 | 
552 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
553 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
554 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     curNode = n;
558 |     pushScope(n);
559 | 
560 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
561 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
562 |       // scope.
563 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
564 |     }
565 | 
566 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
567 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
568 | 
569 |     // Args
570 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
571 | 
572 |     // Body
573 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null && body.isBlock(), body);
574 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
575 | 
576 |     popScope();
577 |   }

718 |       Node node = newNode(transformTokenType(statementNode.getType()));
719 |       node.addChildToBack(transform(statementNode.getExpression()));

721 |     }

===== M0046 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 81-105
Documentation:

Method code:
81 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
82 |     try {
83 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
84 |       ParserRunner.ParseResult result = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
85 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
86 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
87 |           logger_);
88 |       root = result.ast;
89 |       compiler.setOldParseTree(sourceFile.getName(), result.oldAst);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
96 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {
99 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
103 |     // the source file and module.
104 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
105 |   }

   * Casts this to an ObjectType, or returns null if this is not an ObjectType.

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)

Original range: 1278-1289
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1278 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1279 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1280 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1281 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1282 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1283 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1284 |       } else {
1285 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1286 |       }
1287 |     }
1288 |     return builder.build();
1289 |   }

===== M0079 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processTryStatement(TryStatement)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 1145-1178
Documentation:

Method code:
1145 |     @Override
1146 |     Node processTryStatement(TryStatement statementNode) {
1147 |       Node node = newNode(Token.TRY,
1148 |           transformBlock(statementNode.getTryBlock()));
1149 |       Node block = newNode(Token.BLOCK);
1150 |       boolean lineSet = false;
1151 | 
1152 |       for (CatchClause cc : statementNode.getCatchClauses()) {
1153 |         // Mark the enclosing block at the same line as the first catch
1154 |         // clause.
1155 |         if (lineSet == false) {
1156 |           block.setLineno(cc.getLineno());
1157 |           maybeSetLengthFrom(block, cc);
1158 |           lineSet = true;
1159 |         }
1160 |         block.addChildToBack(transform(cc));
1161 |       }
1162 |       node.addChildToBack(block);
1163 | 
1164 |       AstNode finallyBlock = statementNode.getFinallyBlock();
1165 |       if (finallyBlock != null) {
1166 |         node.addChildToBack(transformBlock(finallyBlock));
1167 |       }
1168 | 
1169 |       // If we didn't set the line on the catch clause, then
1170 |       // we've got an empty catch clause.  Set its line to be the same
1171 |       // as the finally block (to match Old Rhino's behavior.)
1172 |       if ((lineSet == false) && (finallyBlock != null)) {
1173 |         block.setLineno(finallyBlock.getLineno());
1174 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(block, finallyBlock);
1175 |       }
1176 | 
1177 |       return node;
1178 |     }

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
201 |   boolean parse() {
202 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
203 |     skipEOLs();
204 | 
205 |     JsDocToken token = next();
206 | 
207 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
208 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
209 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
210 |       token = blockInfo.token;
211 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
212 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
213 |       }
214 |     } else {
215 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
216 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
217 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
218 |         // what it was.
219 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
220 |       }
221 |     }
222 | 
223 |     return parseHelperLoop(token, Lists.<ExtendedTypeInfo>newArrayList());
224 |   }

===== M0113 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 123-177
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
123 |   private void scanVars(Node n) {
124 |     switch (n.getType()) {
125 |       case Token.VAR:
126 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
127 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
128 |              child != null;) {
129 |           Node next = child.getNext();
130 |           declareVar(child);
131 |           child = next;
132 |         }
133 |         return;
134 | 
135 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
136 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 | 
140 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
141 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
142 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
146 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
147 | 
148 |       case Token.CATCH:
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
151 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
152 |         // is the code block
153 | 
154 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
155 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
156 | 
157 |         declareVar(var);
158 |         scanVars(block);
159 |         return;  // only one child to scan
160 | 
161 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
162 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
163 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
164 |         break;
165 |     }
166 | 
167 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
168 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
169 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
170 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
171 |            child != null;) {
172 |         Node next = child.getNext();
173 |         scanVars(child);
174 |         child = next;
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 |   }

===== M0116 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 94-155
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
94 |   @Override
95 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
96 |     SubclassRelationship relationship =
97 |         super.getClassesDefinedByCall(callNode);
98 |     if (relationship != null) {
99 |       return relationship;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
103 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
104 |     if (type != null) {
105 |       Node subclass = null;
106 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
107 | 
108 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
109 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
110 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
111 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
112 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
113 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
114 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
115 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
116 |           callName.isGetProp();
117 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
118 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
119 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
120 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
121 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
122 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
123 |       } else {
124 |         return null;
125 |       }
126 | 
127 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
128 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
129 |         // inheritance.
130 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
131 |           return null;
132 |         }
133 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
134 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
135 |             return null;
136 |           }
137 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
138 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
139 |         }
140 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
141 |       }
142 | 
143 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
144 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
145 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
146 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
147 |       if (subclass != null &&
148 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
149 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
150 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
151 |       }
152 |     }
153 | 
154 |     return null;
155 |   }

/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
630 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
631 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
632 |     if (typeSet == null) {
633 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
634 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
638 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
639 | 
640 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
641 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
642 |   }

===== M0129 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1424-1460
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1424 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1425 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1426 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1427 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1428 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1429 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1430 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1431 |           continue;
1432 |         }
1433 |       }
1434 | 
1435 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1436 | 
1437 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1438 |       if (n == null) {
1439 |         continue;
1440 |       }
1441 | 
1442 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1443 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1444 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1445 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1446 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1447 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1448 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1449 | 
1450 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1451 | 
1452 |         externs.add(input);
1453 |         staleInputs = true;
1454 |       }
1455 |     }
1456 | 
1457 |     if (staleInputs) {
1458 |       repartitionInputs();
1459 |     }
1460 |   }

===== M0131 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.TemplateTypeMap.checkEquivalenceHelper(TemplateTypeMap,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/TemplateTypeMap.java
Original range: 196-243
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
196 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
197 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
198 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thisKeys = getTemplateKeys();
199 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thatKeys = that.getTemplateKeys();
200 | 
201 |     EquivalenceMatch[] thatMatches = new EquivalenceMatch[thatKeys.size()];
202 |     Arrays.fill(thatMatches, EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH);
203 | 
204 |     for (int i = 0; i < thisKeys.size(); i++) {
205 |       TemplateType thisKey = thisKeys.get(i);
206 |       JSType thisType = getResolvedTemplateType(thisKey);
207 |       EquivalenceMatch thisMatch = EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH;
208 | 
209 |       for (int j = 0; j < thatKeys.size(); j++) {
210 |         TemplateType thatKey = thatKeys.get(j);
211 |         JSType thatType = that.getResolvedTemplateType(thatKey);
212 | 
213 |         // Cross-compare every key-value pair in this TemplateTypeMap with
214 |         // those in that TemplateTypeMap. Update the Equivalence match for both
215 |         // key-value pairs involved.
216 |         if (thisKey == thatKey) {
217 |           EquivalenceMatch newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MISMATCH;
218 |           if (thisType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatType, eqMethod)) {
219 |             newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH;
220 |           }
221 | 
222 |           if (thisMatch != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
223 |             thisMatch = newMatchType;
224 |           }
225 |           if (thatMatches[j] != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
226 |             thatMatches[j] = newMatchType;
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 |       }
230 | 
231 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thisMatch, eqMethod)) {
232 |         return false;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     for (int i = 0; i < thatMatches.length; i++) {
237 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thatMatches[i], eqMethod)) {
238 |         return false;
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     return true;
243 |   }

===== M0138 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.handleGet(JSModule,Scope,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 633-692
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our representation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name.
     *
     * @param module The current module
     * @param scope The current scope
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     */
Method code:
633 |     void handleGet(JSModule module, Scope scope,
634 |         Node n, Node parent, String name) {
635 |       if (maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(module, scope, n, parent, name)) {
636 |         return;
637 |       }
638 | 
639 |       Ref.Type type = Ref.Type.DIRECT_GET;
640 |       if (parent != null) {
641 |         switch (parent.getType()) {
642 |           case Token.IF:
643 |           case Token.INSTANCEOF:
644 |           case Token.TYPEOF:
645 |           case Token.VOID:
646 |           case Token.NOT:
647 |           case Token.BITNOT:
648 |           case Token.POS:
649 |           case Token.NEG:
650 |             break;
651 |           case Token.CALL:
652 |             if (n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
653 |               // It is a call target
654 |               type = Ref.Type.CALL_GET;
655 |             } else if (isClassDefiningCall(parent)) {
656 |               type = Ref.Type.DIRECT_GET;
657 |             } else {
658 |               type = Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
659 |             }
660 |             break;
661 |           case Token.NEW:
662 |             type = n == parent.getFirstChild()
663 |                    ? Ref.Type.DIRECT_GET
664 |                    : Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
665 |             break;
666 |           case Token.OR:
667 |           case Token.AND:
668 |             // This node is x or y in (x||y) or (x&&y). We only know that an
669 |             // alias is not getting created for this name if the result is used
670 |             // in a boolean context or assigned to the same name
671 |             // (e.g. var a = a || {}).
672 |             type = determineGetTypeForHookOrBooleanExpr(module, scope, parent, name);
673 |             break;
674 |           case Token.HOOK:
675 |             if (n != parent.getFirstChild()) {
676 |               // This node is y or z in (x?y:z). We only know that an alias is
677 |               // not getting created for this name if the result is assigned to
678 |               // the same name (e.g. var a = a ? a : {}).
679 |               type = determineGetTypeForHookOrBooleanExpr(module, scope, parent, name);
680 |             }
681 |             break;
682 |           case Token.DELPROP:
683 |             type = Ref.Type.DELETE_PROP;
684 |             break;
685 |           default:
686 |             type = Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
687 |             break;
688 |         }
689 |       }
690 | 
691 |       handleGet(module, scope, n, parent, name, type);
692 |     }

120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:

153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.handleSetFromGlobal(JSModule,Scope,Node,Node,String,boolean,Name.Type)

Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our representation of the global namespace to reflect an
     * assignment to a global name in global scope.
     *
     * @param module the current module
     * @param scope the current scope
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @param isPropAssign Whether this set corresponds to a property
     *     assignment of the form <code>a.b.c = ...;</code>
     * @param type The type of the value that the name is being assigned
     */
Method code:
547 |     void handleSetFromGlobal(JSModule module, Scope scope,
548 |         Node n, Node parent, String name,
549 |         boolean isPropAssign, Name.Type type) {
550 |       if (maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(module, scope, n, parent, name)) {
551 |         return;
552 |       }
553 | 
554 |       Name nameObj = getOrCreateName(name);
555 |       nameObj.type = type;
556 | 
557 |       Ref set = new Ref(module, scope, n, nameObj, Ref.Type.SET_FROM_GLOBAL,
558 |           currentPreOrderIndex++);
559 |       nameObj.addRef(set);
560 | 
561 |       if (isNestedAssign(parent)) {
562 |         // This assignment is both a set and a get that creates an alias.
563 |         Ref get = new Ref(module, scope, n, nameObj, Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET,
564 |             currentPreOrderIndex++);
565 |         nameObj.addRef(get);
566 |         Ref.markTwins(set, get);
567 |       } else if (isTypeDeclaration(n, parent)) {
568 |         // Names with a @constructor or @enum annotation are always collapsed
569 |         nameObj.setDeclaredType();
570 |       }
571 |     }

===== M0160 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1974-1976
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1974 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1975 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1976 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseAnnotation(JsDocToken,List<ExtendedTypeInfo>)

Method code:
273 |   private JsDocToken parseAnnotation(JsDocToken token,
274 |       List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes) {
275 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
276 |     JSTypeExpression type;
277 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
278 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
279 | 
280 |     String annotationName = stream.getString();
281 |     Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
282 |     if (annotation == null) {
283 |       parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
284 |           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
285 |     } else {
286 |       // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
287 |       jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
288 | 
289 |       switch (annotation) {
290 |         case NG_INJECT:
291 |           if (jsdocBuilder.isNgInjectRecorded()) {
292 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nginject.extra",
293 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
294 |           } else {
295 |             jsdocBuilder.recordNgInject(true);
296 |           }
297 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
298 | 
299 |         case AUTHOR:
300 |           if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
301 |             ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
302 |             String author = authorInfo.string;
303 | 
304 |             if (author.length() == 0) {
305 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
306 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
307 |             } else {
308 |               jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
309 |             }
310 |             token = authorInfo.token;
311 |           } else {
312 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
313 |           }
314 |           return token;
315 | 
316 |         case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
317 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
318 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
319 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
320 |           }
321 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
322 | 
323 |         case STRUCT:
324 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordStruct()) {
325 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
326 |                                   stream.getLineno(),
327 |                                   stream.getCharno());
328 |           }
329 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
330 | 
331 |         case DICT:
332 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDict()) {
333 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
334 |                                   stream.getLineno(),
335 |                                   stream.getCharno());
336 |           }
337 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
338 | 
339 |         case CONSTRUCTOR:
340 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
341 |             if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
342 |               parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
343 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
344 |             } else {
345 |               parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
346 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
347 |             }
348 |           }
349 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
350 | 
351 |         case DEPRECATED:
352 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
353 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
354 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
355 |           }
356 | 
357 |           // Find the reason/description, if any.
358 |           ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
359 |               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
360 | 
361 |           String reason = reasonInfo.string;
362 | 
363 |           if (reason.length() > 0) {
364 |             jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
365 |           }
366 | 
367 |           token = reasonInfo.token;
368 |           return token;
369 |

373 |               parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",

524 |               }

527 |               parser.addTypeWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
528 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
529 |             }

532 |           }

534 |           return token;

538 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
539 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
540 |           }

613 |           skipEOLs();

615 |           lineno = stream.getLineno();
616 |           charno = stream.getCharno();

624 |               // parsing error reported during recursive descent
625 |               // recovering parsing

666 |               // recovering parsing

671 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
672 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
673 |           }

677 |           if (isBracketedParam) {
678 |             token = next();
679 |           }

682 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
683 |                 lineno, charno);

691 |               // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type

694 |                 token = next();

697 |                 }

702 |               } else if (type != null) {

704 |                 // already.
705 |                 type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
706 |               }
707 |             }
708 | 
709 |             // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
710 |             // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
711 |             // for handling properties of params.
712 |             if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
713 |               name = null;
714 |             } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
715 |               if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
716 |                 parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
717 |                     lineno, charno);
718 |               } else {
719 |                 parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
720 |                     lineno, charno);
721 |               }

727 |             return token;
728 |           }

731 | 
732 |           // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
733 |           if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
734 |             ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
735 |                 extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
736 | 
737 |             String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
738 | 
739 |             if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
740 |               jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
741 |                   paramDescription);
742 |             }
743 | 
744 |             token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
745 |           } else {
746 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
747 |           }
748 |           return token;
749 | 
750 |         case PRESERVE_TRY:
751 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
752 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
753 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
754 |           }
755 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
756 | 
757 |         case NO_SHADOW:
758 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {

761 |           }

768 |           }

912 |           // recovering parsing
913 |           boolean hasError = type == null && !canSkipTypeAnnotation;
914 |           if (!hasError) {
915 |             // Record types for @type.
916 |             // If the @private, @protected, or @public annotations
917 |             // have a type attached, pretend that they actually wrote:
918 |             // @type {type}\n@private

920 |             // (for example, @private can now be used as a type-cast),
921 |             // but should be mostly OK.

923 |                 || annotation == Annotation.TYPE) {
924 |               if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
925 |                 parser.addTypeWarning(
926 |                     "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
927 |               }
928 |             }
929 | 
930 |             switch (annotation) {
931 |               case CONSTANT:
932 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
933 |                   parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
934 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
935 |                 }
936 |                 break;
937 | 
938 |               case DEFINE:
939 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
940 |                   parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
941 |                       lineno, charno);
942 |                 }

944 | 
945 |               case PRIVATE:

947 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
948 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
949 |                       lineno, charno);
950 |                 }
951 |                 return recordDescription(token);
952 | 
953 |               case PROTECTED:
954 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
955 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
956 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
957 |                       lineno, charno);
958 |                 }

960 | 
961 |               case PUBLIC:

963 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
964 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
965 |                       lineno, charno);
966 |                 }
967 |                 return recordDescription(token);
968 | 
969 |               case RETURN:

972 |                 }

998 | 
999 |               case THIS:
1000 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
1001 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
1002 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
1003 |                 }

1009 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
1010 |                 }
1011 |                 break;

1013 |           }
1014 | 
1015 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
1016 |       }
1017 |     }

===== M0176 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.collect(JSModule,Scope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 306-430
Documentation:

Method code:
306 |     public void collect(JSModule module, Scope scope, Node n) {
307 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
308 | 
309 |       String name;
310 |       boolean isSet = false;
311 |       Name.Type type = Name.Type.OTHER;
312 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
313 | 
314 |       switch (n.getType()) {
315 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
316 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
317 |         case Token.STRING_KEY:
318 |           // This may be a key in an object literal declaration.
319 |           name = null;
320 |           if (parent != null && parent.isObjectLit()) {
321 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(n);
322 |           }
323 |           if (name == null) {
324 |             return;
325 |           }
326 |           isSet = true;
327 |           switch (n.getType()) {
328 |             case Token.STRING_KEY:
329 |               type = getValueType(n.getFirstChild());
330 |               break;
331 |             case Token.GETTER_DEF:
332 |               type = Name.Type.GET;
333 |               break;
334 |             case Token.SETTER_DEF:
335 |               type = Name.Type.SET;
336 |               break;
337 |             default:
338 |               throw new IllegalStateException("unexpected:" + n);
339 |           }
340 |           break;
341 |         case Token.NAME:
342 |           // This may be a variable get or set.
343 |           if (parent != null) {
344 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
345 |               case Token.VAR:
346 |                 isSet = true;
347 |                 Node rvalue = n.getFirstChild();
348 |                 type = rvalue == null ? Name.Type.OTHER : getValueType(rvalue);
349 |                 break;
350 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
351 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
352 |                   isSet = true;
353 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
354 |                 }
355 |                 break;
356 |               case Token.GETPROP:
357 |                 return;
358 |               case Token.FUNCTION:
359 |                 Node gramps = parent.getParent();
360 |                 if (gramps == null || NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
361 |                   return;
362 |                 }
363 |                 isSet = true;
364 |                 type = Name.Type.FUNCTION;
365 |                 break;
366 |               case Token.INC:
367 |               case Token.DEC:
368 |                 isSet = true;
369 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
370 |                 break;
371 |               default:
372 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
373 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
374 |                   isSet = true;
375 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
376 |                 }
377 |             }
378 |           }
379 |           name = n.getString();
380 |           break;
381 |         case Token.GETPROP:
382 |           // This may be a namespaced name get or set.
383 |           if (parent != null) {
384 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
385 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
386 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
387 |                   isSet = true;
388 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
389 |                   isPropAssign = true;
390 |                 }
391 |                 break;
392 |               case Token.INC:
393 |               case Token.DEC:
394 |                 isSet = true;
395 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
396 |                 break;
397 |               case Token.GETPROP:
398 |                 return;
399 |               default:
400 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
401 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
402 |                   isSet = true;
403 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
404 |                 }
405 |             }
406 |           }
407 |           name = n.getQualifiedName();
408 |           if (name == null) {
409 |             return;
410 |           }
411 |           break;
412 |         default:
413 |           return;
414 |       }
415 | 
416 |       // We are only interested in global names.
417 |       if (!isGlobalNameReference(name, scope)) {
418 |         return;
419 |       }
420 | 
421 |       if (isSet) {
422 |         if (isGlobalScope(scope)) {
423 |           handleSetFromGlobal(module, scope, n, parent, name, isPropAssign, type);
424 |         } else {
425 |           handleSetFromLocal(module, scope, n, parent, name);
426 |         }
427 |       } else {
428 |         handleGet(module, scope, n, parent, name);
429 |       }
430 |     }

===== M0177 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1193-1201
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1193 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1194 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1195 |       return -1;
1196 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1197 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1198 |     } else {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1200 |     }
1201 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()

/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
57 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
58 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
59 |     int c;
60 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
61 |     for (;;) {
62 |       // eat white spaces
63 |       for (;;) {
64 |         charno = -1;
65 |         c = getChar();
66 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
67 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
68 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
70 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
71 |           break;
72 |         }
73 |       }
74 | 
75 |       switch (c) {
76 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
77 |         case '@':
78 |           do {
79 |             c = getChar();
80 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
81 |               addToString(c);
82 |             } else {
83 |               ungetChar(c);
84 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
85 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
86 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
87 |             }
88 |           } while (true);
89 | 
90 |         case '*':
91 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
92 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
93 |           } else {
94 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
95 |           }
96 | 
97 |         case ',':
98 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
99 | 
100 |         case '>':
101 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
102 | 
103 |         case '(':
104 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
105 | 
106 |         case ')':
107 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
108 | 
109 |         case '{':
110 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
111 | 
112 |         case '}':
113 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
114 | 
115 |         case '[':
116 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
117 | 
118 |         case ']':
119 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
120 | 
121 |         case '?':
122 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
123 | 
124 |         case '!':
125 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
126 | 
127 |         case ':':
128 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
129 | 
130 |         case '=':
131 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
132 | 
133 |         case '|':
134 |           matchChar('|');
135 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
136 | 
137 |         case '.':
138 |           c = getChar();
139 |           if (c == '<') {
140 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
141 |           } else {
142 |             if (c == '.') {
143 |               c = getChar();
144 |               if (c == '.') {
145 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
146 |               } else {
147 |                 addToString('.');
148 |               }
149 |             }
150 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
151 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
152 |             c = '.';
153 |           }
154 |           // fall through
155 | 
156 |         default: {
157 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
158 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
159 |           int c1 = c;
160 |           addToString(c);
161 |           int c2 = getChar();
162 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
163 |             ungetChar(c2);
164 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
165 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
166 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
167 |           } else {
168 |             do {
169 |               c1 = c2;
170 |               c2 = getChar();
171 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
172 |                 ungetChar(c2);
173 |                 ungetChar(c1);
174 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
175 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
176 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
177 |               } else {
178 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
179 |                   addToString(c1);
180 |                 } else {
181 |                   ungetChar(c2);
182 |                   addToString(c1);
183 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
184 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
185 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
186 |                 }
187 |               }
188 |             } while (true);
189 |           }
190 |         }
191 |       }
192 |     }
193 |   }

Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
308 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
309 |     if (c <= 127) {
310 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
311 |     } else {
312 |       return c == 0xA0
313 |           || Character.getType((char) c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
314 |     }
315 |   }

===== M0191 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 335-389
Documentation:

Method code:
335 |   protected int getChar() {
336 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
337 |       cursor++;
338 |       --ungetCursor;
339 |       if (charno == -1) {
340 |         charno = getOffset();
341 |       }
342 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
343 |     }
344 | 
345 |     for (;;) {
346 |       int c;
347 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
348 |         hitEOF = true;
349 |         if (charno == -1) {
350 |           charno = getOffset();
351 |         }
352 |         return EOF_CHAR;
353 |       }
354 |       cursor++;
355 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
356 | 
357 | 
358 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
359 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
360 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
361 |           continue;
362 |         }
363 |         lineEndChar = -1;
364 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
365 |         lineno++;
366 |       }
367 | 
368 |       if (c <= 127) {
369 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
370 |           lineEndChar = c;
371 |           c = '\n';
372 |         }
373 |       } else {
374 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
375 |           continue;
376 |         }
377 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
378 |           lineEndChar = c;
379 |           c = '\n';
380 |         }
381 |       }
382 | 
383 |       if (charno == -1) {
384 |         charno = getOffset();
385 |       }
386 | 
387 |       return c;
388 |     }
389 |   }

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
796 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
797 |     if (item == null) {
798 |       return null;
799 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
800 |       return item.getNext();
801 |     } else {
802 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
803 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
804 |         return item.chain(result);
805 |       } else {
806 |         return item;
807 |       }
808 |     }
809 |   }

===== M0206 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java

Documentation:

Method code:
391 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
392 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
393 |       cursor++;
394 |       --ungetCursor;
395 |       if (charno == -1) {
396 |         charno = getOffset();
397 |       }
398 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
399 |     }
400 | 
401 |     for (;;) {
402 |       int c;
403 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
404 |         hitEOF = true;
405 |         if (charno == -1) {
406 |           charno = getOffset();
407 |         }
408 |         return EOF_CHAR;
409 |       }
410 |       cursor++;
411 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
412 | 
413 | 
414 |       if (c <= 127) {
415 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
416 |           lineEndChar = c;
417 |           c = '\n';
418 |         }
419 |       } else {
420 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
421 |           continue;
422 |         }
423 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
424 |           lineEndChar = c;
425 |           c = '\n';
426 |         }
427 |       }
428 | 
429 |       if (charno == -1) {
430 |         charno = getOffset();
431 |       }
432 | 
433 |       return c;
434 |     }
435 |   }

   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
552 |   @Override
553 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
554 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
555 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
556 |     if (unknown) {
557 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
558 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
559 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
560 |         unknown = false;
561 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
562 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
563 |             unknown = true;
564 |             break;
565 |           }
566 |         }
567 |       } else {
568 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
569 |       }
570 |     }
571 |     return unknown;
572 |   }

===== M0220 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1514-1565
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recur Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @param shallow If true, the method doesn't recur into inner functions.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1514 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(
1515 |       Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recur, boolean shallow) {
1516 |     if (type != node.getType()
1517 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1518 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1519 |       return false;
1520 |     }
1521 | 
1522 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1523 |       return false;
1524 |     }
1525 | 
1526 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1527 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1528 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1529 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1530 |         return false;
1531 |       }
1532 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1533 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1534 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1535 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1536 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1537 |           return false;
1538 |         }
1539 |       }
1540 | 
1541 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1542 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1543 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1544 |         return false;
1545 |       }
1546 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1547 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1548 |         return false;
1549 |       }
1550 |     }
1551 | 
1552 |     if (recur) {
1553 |       Node n, n2;
1554 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1555 |            n != null;
1556 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1557 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(
1558 |             n2, compareJsType, !(shallow && n.isFunction()), shallow)) {
1559 |           return false;
1560 |         }
1561 |       }
1562 |     }
1563 | 
1564 |     return true;
1565 |   }

/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @nocompile annotation out of the inputs.
   */
Method code:
1465 |   private void hoistNoCompileFiles() {
1466 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1467 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1468 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1469 | 
1470 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1471 |       if (n == null) {
1472 |         continue;
1473 |       }
1474 | 
1475 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1476 |       if (info != null && info.isNoCompile()) {
1477 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1478 |         staleInputs = true;
1479 |       }
1480 |     }
1481 | 
1482 |     if (staleInputs) {
1483 |       repartitionInputs();
1484 |     }
1485 |   }

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 313-352
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
313 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
314 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
315 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
316 |       options.checkTypes = true;
317 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
318 |       options.checkTypes = false;
319 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
320 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
321 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
322 |       // parser type warnings.
323 |       options.setWarningLevel(
324 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
325 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
326 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
330 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
331 |       options.setWarningLevel(
332 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
333 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
337 |       options.setWarningLevel(
338 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
339 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
340 |     }
341 | 
342 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
343 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
344 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
345 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
346 |     // error off.
347 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
348 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
349 |       options.setWarningLevel(
350 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
351 |     }
352 |   }

===== M0298 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 83-118
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   private void scanRoot(Node n) {
84 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
85 |       if (inputId == null) {
86 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
87 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
88 |         // from the AST.
89 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
90 |       }
91 | 
92 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
93 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
94 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
95 | 
96 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
97 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
98 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
99 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
100 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
101 |       }
102 | 
103 |       // Args: Declare function variables
104 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
105 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
106 |            a = a.getNext()) {
107 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
108 |         declareVar(a);
109 |       }
110 | 
111 |       // Body
112 |       scanVars(body);
113 |     } else {
114 |       // It's the global block
115 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
116 |       scanVars(n);
117 |     }
118 |   }

1718 |   public Node cloneTree() {

62 |       int priorityDiff = a.getPriority() - b.getPriority();
63 |       if (priorityDiff != 0) {
64 |         return priorityDiff;
65 |       }
66 | 
67 |       // If the warnings guards have the same priority, the one that
68 |       // was added last wins.
69 |       return orderOfAddition.get(b).intValue() -
70 |           orderOfAddition.get(a).intValue();
71 |     }

===== M0343 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getValueType(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 508-532
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the type of a value or simple expression.
     *
     * @param n An r-value in an assignment or variable declaration (not null)
     * @return A {@link Name.Type}
     */
Method code:
508 |     Name.Type getValueType(Node n) {
509 |       switch (n.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
511 |           return Name.Type.OBJECTLIT;
512 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
513 |           return Name.Type.FUNCTION;
514 |         case Token.OR:
515 |           // Recurse on the second value. If the first value were an object
516 |           // literal or function, then the OR would be meaningless and the
517 |           // second value would be dead code. Assume that if the second value
518 |           // is an object literal or function, then the first value will also
519 |           // evaluate to one when it doesn't evaluate to false.
520 |           return getValueType(n.getLastChild());
521 |         case Token.HOOK:
522 |           // The same line of reasoning used for the OR case applies here.
523 |           Node second = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
524 |           Name.Type t = getValueType(second);
525 |           if (t != Name.Type.OTHER) {
526 |             return t;
527 |           }
528 |           Node third = second.getNext();
529 |           return getValueType(third);
530 |       }
531 |       return Name.Type.OTHER;
532 |     }

===== M0353 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

===== M0361 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 548-561
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
548 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
549 |       Node ret;
550 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
551 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name) n);
552 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
553 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral) n);
554 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
555 |       } else {
556 |         ret = transform(n);
557 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
558 |       }
559 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
560 |       return ret;
561 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 632-646
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
632 |   public Scope getScope() {
633 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
634 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
635 |       return scope;
636 |     }
637 | 
638 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
639 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
640 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
641 |       scopes.push(scope);
642 |     }
643 |     scopeRoots.clear();
644 |     // No need to call compiler.setScope; the top scopeRoot is now the top scope
645 |     return scope;
