===== M0384 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1295-1419
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1295 |   Node parseInputs() {
1296 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1297 | 
1298 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1299 |     // individual file parse trees.
1300 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1301 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1302 |     }
1303 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1304 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1305 |     }
1306 | 
1307 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1308 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1309 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1310 | 
1311 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1312 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1313 | 
1314 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1315 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1316 | 
1317 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1318 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1319 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1320 |     }
1321 | 
1322 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer(PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1323 | 
1324 |     try {
1325 |       // Parse externs sources.
1326 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1327 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1328 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1329 |           return null;
1330 |         }
1331 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1332 |       }
1333 | 
1334 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1335 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1336 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1337 |       }
1338 | 
1339 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1340 | 
1341 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1342 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1343 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1344 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1345 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1346 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1347 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1348 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1349 |           }
1350 |         }
1351 | 
1352 |         try {
1353 |           inputs =
1354 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1355 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1356 |           staleInputs = true;
1357 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1358 |           report(JSError.make(
1359 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1360 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1361 |           report(JSError.make(
1362 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1363 |         } catch (JSModuleGraph.MissingModuleException e) {
1364 |           report(JSError.make(
1365 |               MISSING_MODULE_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1366 |         }
1367 | 
1368 |         // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1369 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1370 |           return null;
1371 |         }
1372 |       }
1373 | 
1374 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1375 | 
1376 |       if (staleInputs) {
1377 |         repartitionInputs();
1378 |       }
1379 | 
1380 |       // Build the AST.
1381 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1382 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1383 |         if (n == null) {
1384 |           continue;
1385 |         }
1386 | 
1387 |         if (devMode) {
1388 |           runSanityCheck();
1389 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1390 |             return null;
1391 |           }
1392 |         }
1393 | 
1394 |         // TODO(johnlenz): we shouldn't need to check both isExternExportsEnabled and
1395 |         // externExportsPath.
1396 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1397 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null ||
1398 |             options.isExternExportsEnabled() ||
1399 |             options.externExportsPath != null) {
1400 | 
1401 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1402 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1403 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1404 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1405 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1406 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1407 |         }
1408 | 
1409 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1410 |       }
1411 | 
1412 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1413 |         return null;
1414 |       }
1415 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1416 |     } finally {
1417 |       stopTracer(tracer, PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1418 |     }
1419 |   }

===== M0398 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1236-1296
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1236 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1237 |     // unknown
1238 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1239 |       return true;
1240 |     }
1241 |     // all type
1242 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1243 |       return true;
1244 |     }
1245 |     // equality
1246 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1247 |       return true;
1248 |     }
1249 |     // unions
1250 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1251 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1252 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1253 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1254 |           return true;
1255 |         }
1256 |       }
1257 |       return false;
1258 |     }
1259 | 
1260 |     // TemplateTypeMaps. This check only returns false if the TemplateTypeMaps
1261 |     // are not equivalent.
1262 |     TemplateTypeMap thisTypeParams = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1263 |     TemplateTypeMap thatTypeParams = thatType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1264 |     boolean templateMatch = true;
1265 |     if (isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType)) {
1266 |       // Array and Object are exempt from template type invariance; their
1267 |       // template types maps are considered a match only if the ObjectElementKey
1268 |       // values are subtypes/supertypes of one another.
1269 |       TemplateType key = thisType.registry.getObjectElementKey();
1270 |       JSType thisElement = thisTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1271 |       JSType thatElement = thatTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1272 | 
1273 |       templateMatch = thisElement.isSubtype(thatElement)
1274 |           || thatElement.isSubtype(thisElement);
1275 |     } else {
1276 |       templateMatch = thisTypeParams.checkEquivalenceHelper(
1277 |           thatTypeParams, EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT);
1278 |     }
1279 |     if (!templateMatch) {
1280 |       return false;
1281 |     }
1282 | 
1283 |     // Templatized types. The above check guarantees TemplateTypeMap
1284 |     // equivalence; check if the base type is a subtype.
1285 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1286 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1287 |               thatType);
1288 |     }
1289 | 
1290 |     // proxy types
1291 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1292 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1293 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1294 |     }
1295 |     return false;
1296 |   }

787 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
788 |     }

===== M0411 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1228-1230
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1228 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1229 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1230 |   }

===== M0421 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-101
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       boolean visitedFunction = false;
56 |       for (Node current = node;
57 |            current != null;
58 |            current = current.getParent()) {
59 |         int type = current.getType();
60 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
61 | 
62 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
63 |           info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(current);
64 |           visitedFunction = true;
65 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
66 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
67 |         } else if (current.isVar() || current.isAssign()) {
68 |           // There's one edge case we're worried about:
69 |           // if the warning points to an assigment to a function, we
70 |           // want the suppressions on that function to apply.
71 |           // It's OK if we double-count some cases.
72 |           Node rhs = NodeUtil.getRValueOfLValue(current.getFirstChild());
73 |           if (rhs != null) {
74 |             if (rhs.isCast()) {
75 |               rhs = rhs.getFirstChild();
76 |             }
77 | 
78 |             if (rhs.isFunction() && !visitedFunction) {
79 |               info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(current);
80 |             }
81 |           }
82 |         }
83 | 
84 |         if (info != null) {
85 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
86 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
87 | 
88 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
89 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
90 |             if (guard != null) {
91 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
92 |               if (newLevel != null) {
93 |                 return newLevel;
94 |               }
95 |             }
96 |           }
97 |         }
98 |       }
99 |     }
100 |     return null;
101 |   }

/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.

   * For the purposes of this function, we define a MaybeFunctionType as any
   * type in the sub-lattice
   * { x | LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE <= x <= GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE }
   * This definition excludes bottom types like NoType and NoObjectType.
   *
   * This definition is somewhat arbitrary and axiomatic, but this is the
   * definition that makes the most sense for the most callers.

===== M0432 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo,AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 295-354
Documentation:

Method code:
295 |   private void validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo info, AstNode node) {
296 |     if (info.hasType()) {
297 |       boolean valid = false;
298 |       switch (node.getType()) {
299 |         // Casts are valid
300 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.LP:
301 |           valid = node instanceof ParenthesizedExpression;
302 |           break;
303 |         // Variable declarations are valid
304 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.VAR:
305 |           valid = true;
306 |           break;
307 |         // Function declarations are valid
308 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.FUNCTION:
309 |           FunctionNode fnNode = (FunctionNode) node;
310 |           valid = fnNode.getFunctionType() == FunctionNode.FUNCTION_STATEMENT;
311 |           break;
312 |         // Object literal properties, catch declarations and variable
313 |         // initializers are valid.
314 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NAME:
315 |           AstNode parent = node.getParent();
316 |           valid = parent instanceof ObjectProperty
317 |               || parent instanceof CatchClause
318 |               || parent instanceof FunctionNode
319 |               || (parent instanceof VariableInitializer &&
320 |                   node == ((VariableInitializer) parent).getTarget());
321 |           break;
322 |         // Object literal properties are valid
323 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GET:
324 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.SET:
325 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NUMBER:
326 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.STRING:
327 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty;
328 |           break;
329 | 
330 |         // Property assignments are valid, if at the root of an expression.
331 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.ASSIGN:
332 |           if (node instanceof Assignment) {
333 |             valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent())
334 |                 && isPropAccess(((Assignment) node).getLeft());
335 |           }
336 |           break;
337 | 
338 |         // Property definitions are valid, if at the root of an expression.
339 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETPROP:
340 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETELEM:
341 |           valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent());
342 |           break;
343 | 
344 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.CALL:
345 |           valid = info.isDefine();
346 |           break;
347 |       }
348 |       if (!valid) {
349 |         errorReporter.warning(MISPLACED_TYPE_ANNOTATION,
350 |             sourceName,
351 |             node.getLineno(), "", 0);
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 |   }

===== M0433 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 399-424
Documentation:

Method code:
399 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
400 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
401 |     // two cases:
402 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
403 |     // and
404 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
405 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
406 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
407 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
408 |     //
409 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
410 |     //
411 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
412 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
413 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
414 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
415 |     //
416 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
417 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
418 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
419 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
420 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
421 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
422 |     }
423 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
424 |   }

===== M0446 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 157-179
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
157 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
158 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
159 | 
160 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
161 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
162 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
163 | 
164 |       // ignore cast nodes.
165 |       while (first.isCast()) {
166 |         first = first.getFirstChild();
167 |       }
168 | 
169 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
170 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
171 |       }
172 | 
173 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
174 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
175 |       if (first.isName() &&
176 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
177 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
178 |       }
179 |     }

===== M0463 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken,List<ExtendedTypeInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 226-271
Documentation:

Method code:
226 |   private boolean parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken token,
227 |                                   List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes) {
228 |     while (true) {
229 |       switch (token) {
230 |         case ANNOTATION:
231 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
232 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
233 |             token = parseAnnotation(token, extendedTypes);
234 |           } else {
235 |             token = next();
236 |           }
237 |           break;
238 | 
239 |         case EOC:
240 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
241 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
242 |           }
243 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
244 |           return true;
245 | 
246 |         case EOF:
247 |           // discard any accumulated information
248 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
249 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
250 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
252 |           return false;
253 | 
254 |         case EOL:
255 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
256 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
257 |           }
258 |           token = next();
259 |           break;
260 | 
261 |         default:
262 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
263 |             token = next();
264 |           } else {
265 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
266 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
267 |           }
268 |           break;
269 |       }
270 |     }
271 |   }

505 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(

/**
     * Determines whether a set operation is a constructor or enumeration
     * or interface declaration. The set operation may either be an assignment
     * to a name, a variable declaration, or an object literal key mapping.
     *
     * @param n The node that represents the name being set

/**
     * Gets a {@link Name} instance for a global name. Creates it if necessary,
     * as well as instances for any of its prefixes that are not yet defined.
     *
     * @param name A global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c.d")

Method code:
851 |     Name getOrCreateName(String name) {
852 |       Name node = nameMap.get(name);
853 |       if (node == null) {

===== M0487 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 609-624
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
609 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
610 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
611 |       Set<String> directives = null;
612 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
613 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
614 |         if (directives == null) {
615 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
616 |         } else {
617 |           directives.add(directive);
618 |         }
619 |       }
620 | 
621 |       if (directives != null) {
622 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
623 |       }
624 |     }

===== M0490 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 127-178
Documentation:

Method code:
127 |     @Override
128 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
129 |       // null is the smallest value
130 |       if (p2 == null) {
131 |         if (p1 == null) {
132 |           return 0;
133 |         } else {
134 |           return P1_GT_P2;
135 |         }
136 |       }
137 | 
138 |       // check level
139 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
140 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
141 |       }
142 | 
143 |       // sourceName comparison
144 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
145 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
146 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
147 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
148 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
149 |           return sourceCompare;
150 |         }
151 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
152 |         return P1_LT_P2;
153 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
154 |         return P1_GT_P2;
155 |       }
156 |       // lineno comparison
157 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
158 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
159 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
160 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
161 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
162 |         return P1_LT_P2;
163 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
164 |         return P1_GT_P2;
165 |       }
166 |       // charno comparison
167 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
168 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
169 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
170 |         return charno1 - charno2;
171 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
172 |         return P1_LT_P2;
173 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
174 |         return P1_GT_P2;
175 |       }
176 |       // description
177 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
178 |     }

===== M0498 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(JSModule,Scope,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 791-829
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our representation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name's longest prefix before the "prototype" property if the
     * name includes the "prototype" property. Does nothing otherwise.
     *
     * @param module The current module
     * @param scope The current scope
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @return Whether the name was handled
     */
Method code:
791 |     boolean maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(JSModule module, Scope scope,
792 |         Node n, Node parent, String name) {
793 |       // We use a string-based approach instead of inspecting the parse tree
794 |       // to avoid complexities with object literals, possibly nested, beneath
795 |       // assignments.
796 | 
797 |       int numLevelsToRemove;
798 |       String prefix;
799 |       if (name.endsWith(".prototype")) {
800 |         numLevelsToRemove = 1;
801 |         prefix = name.substring(0, name.length() - 10);
802 |       } else {
803 |         int i = name.indexOf(".prototype.");
804 |         if (i == -1) {
805 |           return false;
806 |         }
807 |         prefix = name.substring(0, i);
808 |         numLevelsToRemove = 2;
809 |         i = name.indexOf('.', i + 11);
810 |         while (i >= 0) {
811 |           numLevelsToRemove++;
812 |           i = name.indexOf('.', i + 1);
813 |         }
814 |       }
815 | 
816 |       if (parent != null && NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n)) {
817 |         // Object literal keys have no prefix that's referenced directly per
818 |         // key, so we're done.
819 |         return true;
820 |       }
821 | 
822 |       for (int i = 0; i < numLevelsToRemove; i++) {
823 |         parent = n;
824 |         n = n.getFirstChild();
825 |       }
826 | 
827 |       handleGet(module, scope, n, parent, prefix, Ref.Type.PROTOTYPE_GET);
828 |       return true;
829 |     }

244 |         System.arraycopy(stringBuffer, 0, tmp, 0, n);
245 |         stringBuffer = tmp;
246 |     }
247 |     stringBuffer[n] = (char) c;
248 |     stringBufferTop = n + 1;
249 |   }

===== M0516 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 570-638
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
570 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
571 |     if (this == that) {
572 |       return true;
573 |     }
574 | 
575 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
576 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
577 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
578 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
579 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
580 |         // with everyone.
581 |         return true;
582 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
583 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
584 |         // both unknown.
585 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
586 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
587 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
588 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
589 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
590 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
591 |         return false;
592 |       }
593 |     }
594 | 
595 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
596 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
597 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
598 |     }
599 | 
600 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
601 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
602 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
603 |     }
604 | 
605 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
606 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
607 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
608 |     }
609 | 
610 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
611 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
612 |       return false;
613 |     }
614 | 
615 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
616 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
617 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
618 |     }
619 | 
620 |     // Unbox other proxies.
621 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
622 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
623 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
624 |               that, eqMethod);
625 |     }
626 | 
627 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
628 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
629 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
630 |           eqMethod);
631 |     }
632 | 
633 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
634 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
635 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
636 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
637 |     return this == that;
638 |   }

/**
   * Checks to make sure all the descendants of a name are defined if they
   * are referenced.
   *
   * @param name A global name.
   * @param nameIsDefined If true, {@code name} is defined. Otherwise, it's
   *    undefined, and any references to descendant names should emit warnings.
   */
Method code:
127 |   private void checkDescendantNames(Name name, boolean nameIsDefined) {
128 |     if (name.props != null) {
129 |       for (Name prop : name.props) {
130 |         // if the ancestor of a property is not defined, then we should emit
131 |         // warnings for all references to the property.
132 |         boolean propIsDefined = false;
133 |         if (nameIsDefined) {
134 |           // if the ancestor of a property is defined, then let's check that
135 |           // the property is also explicitly defined if it needs to be.
136 |           propIsDefined = (!propertyMustBeInitializedByFullName(prop) ||
137 |               prop.globalSets + prop.localSets > 0);
138 |         }
139 | 
140 |         validateName(prop, propIsDefined);
141 |         checkDescendantNames(prop, propIsDefined);
142 |       }
143 |     }
144 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 162-183
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
162 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
163 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
164 |     String methodName = null;
165 |     if (callName.isGetProp()) {
166 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
167 |     } else if (callName.isName()) {
168 |       String name = callName.getString();
169 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
170 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
171 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
172 |       }
173 |     }
174 | 
175 |     if (methodName != null) {
176 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
177 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
178 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
179 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
180 |       }
181 |     }
182 |     return null;
183 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1575-1590
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1575 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1576 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1577 |       String name = getString();
1578 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1579 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1580 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1581 |       if (left == null) {
1582 |         return null;
1583 |       }
1584 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1585 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1586 |       return "this";
1587 |     } else {
1588 |       return null;
1589 |     }
1590 |   }

Method code:
69 |   TemplateTypeMap(JSTypeRegistry registry,
70 |                   ImmutableList<TemplateType> templateKeys,
71 |                   ImmutableList<JSType> templateValues) {
72 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(templateKeys);
73 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(templateValues);
74 |

76 |     this.templateKeys = templateKeys;
77 |

79 |     this.templateValues = templateValues.size() > nKeys ?
80 |         templateValues.subList(0, nKeys) : templateValues;

83 |     // of this TemplateTypeMap.

85 |         registry, this);

87 |     for (JSType templateValue : this.templateValues) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateTry(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java

Documentation:

Method code:
510 |   private void validateTry(Node n) {
511 |     validateNodeType(Token.TRY, n);
512 |     validateMinimumChildCount(n, 2);
513 |     validateMaximumChildCount(n, 3);
514 |     validateBlock(n.getFirstChild());
515 | 
516 |     boolean seenCatchOrFinally = false;
517 | 
518 |     // Validate catch
519 |     Node catches = n.getChildAtIndex(1);
520 |     validateNodeType(Token.BLOCK, catches);
521 |     validateMaximumChildCount(catches, 1);
522 |     if (catches.hasChildren()) {
523 |       validateCatch(catches.getFirstChild());
524 |       seenCatchOrFinally = true;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     // Validate finally
528 |     if (n.getChildCount() == 3) {
529 |       validateBlock(n.getLastChild());
530 |       seenCatchOrFinally = true;
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     if (!seenCatchOrFinally) {
534 |       violation("Missing catch or finally for try statement.", n);
535 |     }
536 |   }

===== M0548 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckGlobalNames.validateName(Name,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckGlobalNames.java
Original range: 146-194
Documentation:

Method code:
146 |   private void validateName(Name name, boolean isDefined) {
147 |     // If the name is not defined, emit warnings for each reference. While
148 |     // we're looking through each reference, check all the module dependencies.
149 |     Ref declaration = name.getDeclaration();
150 |     Name parent = name.parent;
151 | 
152 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
153 |     for (Ref ref : name.getRefs()) {
154 |       // Don't worry about global exprs.
155 |       boolean isGlobalExpr = ref.getNode().getParent().isExprResult();
156 | 
157 |       if (!isDefined && !isTypedef(ref)) {
158 |         if (!isGlobalExpr) {
159 |           reportRefToUndefinedName(name, ref);
160 |         }
161 |       } else if (declaration != null &&
162 |           ref.getModule() != declaration.getModule() &&
163 |           !moduleGraph.dependsOn(
164 |               ref.getModule(), declaration.getModule())) {
165 |         reportBadModuleReference(name, ref);
166 |       } else {
167 |         // Check for late references.
168 |         if (ref.scope.isGlobal()) {
169 |           // Prototype references are special, because in our reference graph,
170 |           // A.prototype counts as a reference to A.
171 |           boolean isPrototypeGet = (ref.type == Ref.Type.PROTOTYPE_GET);
172 |           Name owner = isPrototypeGet ? name : parent;
173 |           boolean singleGlobalParentDecl =
174 |               owner != null &&
175 |               owner.getDeclaration() != null &&
176 |               owner.localSets == 0;
177 | 
178 |           if (singleGlobalParentDecl &&
179 |               owner.getDeclaration().preOrderIndex > ref.preOrderIndex) {
180 |             String refName = isPrototypeGet
181 |                 ? name.getFullName() + ".prototype"
182 |                 : name.getFullName();
183 |             compiler.report(
184 |                 JSError.make(ref.source.getName(), ref.node,
185 |                     NAME_DEFINED_LATE_WARNING,
186 |                     refName,
187 |                     owner.getFullName(),
188 |                     owner.getDeclaration().source.getName(),
189 |                     String.valueOf(owner.getDeclaration().node.getLineno())));
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processCatchClause(CatchClause)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 653-665
Documentation:

Method code:
653 |     @Override
654 |     Node processCatchClause(CatchClause clauseNode) {
655 |       AstNode catchVar = clauseNode.getVarName();
656 |       Node node = newNode(Token.CATCH, transform(catchVar));
657 |       if (clauseNode.getCatchCondition() != null) {
658 |         errorReporter.error(
659 |             "Catch clauses are not supported",
660 |             sourceName,
661 |             clauseNode.getCatchCondition().getLineno(), "", 0);
662 |       }
663 |       node.addChildToBack(transformBlock(clauseNode.getBody()));
664 |       return node;
665 |     }

===== M0552 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

/**
   * Create the annotation names from the user-specified
   * annotation whitelist.
   */
Method code:
89 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(
90 |       Set<String> annotationWhitelist) {
91 |     ImmutableMap.Builder<String, Annotation> annotationBuilder =
92 |         ImmutableMap.builder();
93 |     annotationBuilder.putAll(Annotation.recognizedAnnotations);
94 |     for (String unrecognizedAnnotation : annotationWhitelist) {
95 |       if (!Annotation.recognizedAnnotations.containsKey(
96 |               unrecognizedAnnotation)) {

99 |       }
100 |     }

216 |   private void reportRefToUndefinedName(Name name, Ref ref) {
217 |     // grab the highest undefined ancestor to output in the warning message.
218 |     while (name.parent != null &&
219 |            name.parent.globalSets + name.parent.localSets == 0) {
220 |       name = name.parent;
221 |     }
222 | 
223 |     compiler.report(
224 |         JSError.make(ref.getSourceName(), ref.node, level,
225 |             UNDEFINED_NAME_WARNING, name.getFullName()));
226 |   }

===== M0583 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 766-848
Documentation:

Method code:
766 |     @Override
767 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
768 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
769 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
770 |       if (name == null) {
771 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
772 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
773 |           errorReporter.error(
774 |             "unnamed function statement",
775 |             sourceName,
776 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
777 | 
778 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
779 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
780 |         }
781 |         name = new Name();
782 |         name.setIdentifier("");
783 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
784 |       }
785 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
786 |       Node newName = transform(name);
787 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
788 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
789 |         // declaration.
790 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
791 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
792 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
793 |         // same line as the function keyword.
794 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
795 |             functionNode.getLp();
796 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
797 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
798 |       }
799 | 
800 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
801 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
802 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
803 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
804 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
805 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
806 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
807 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
808 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
809 |       if (fnName != null) {
810 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
811 |       } else {
812 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
813 |       }
814 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
815 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
816 | 
817 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
818 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
819 |         Node paramNode = transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(param);
820 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
821 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
822 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
823 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
824 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
825 |         } else {
826 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
827 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
828 |           // (an error has already been reported).
829 |           Preconditions.checkState(
830 |               config.isIdeMode
831 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
832 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
833 |         }
834 |       }
835 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
836 | 
837 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
838 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
839 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
840 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
841 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
842 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
843 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
844 |       }
845 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
846 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
847 |      return node;
848 |     }

===== M0584 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 288-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 529-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
529 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
530 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
531 |          current != null;
532 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
533 |       if (current.isTemplatizedType()) {
534 |         current = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType();
535 |       }
536 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
537 |         return true;
538 |       }
539 |     }
540 |     return false;
541 |   }

Original range: 2378-2387
Documentation:
/**
   * Eats tokens until {@link JsDocToken#EOL} included, and switches back the
   * state to {@link State#SEARCHING_ANNOTATION}.
   */
Method code:
2378 |   private JsDocToken eatTokensUntilEOL(JsDocToken token) {
2379 |     do {
2380 |       if (token == JsDocToken.EOL || token == JsDocToken.EOC ||
2381 |           token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
2382 |         state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
2383 |         return token;
2384 |       }
2385 |       token = next();
2386 |     } while (true);
2387 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
186 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
187 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
188 |                                    String sourceString,
189 |                                    Config config,
190 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
191 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,
192 |         config, errorReporter);
193 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
194 | 
195 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
196 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
197 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.JSDOC &&
198 |             !irFactory.parsedComments.contains(comment)) {
199 |           irFactory.handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(comment, irNode);
200 |         } else if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.BLOCK_COMMENT) {
201 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);
202 |         }
203 |       }
204 |     }
205 | 
206 |     irFactory.setFileOverviewJsDoc(irNode);
207 | 
208 |     return irNode;
209 |   }

1028 |     Node processPropertyGet(PropertyGet getNode) {
1029 |       Node leftChild = transform(getNode.getTarget());
1030 |       AstNode nodeProp = getNode.getProperty();
1031 |       Node rightChild = transformAsString(nodeProp);
1032 |       if (nodeProp instanceof Name && !isAllowedProp(
1033 |           ((Name) nodeProp).getIdentifier())) {
1034 |         errorReporter.warning(INVALID_ES3_PROP_NAME, sourceName,
1035 |             rightChild.getLineno(), "", rightChild.getCharno());
1036 |       }
1037 |       Node newNode = newNode(
1038 |           Token.GETPROP, leftChild, rightChild);
1039 |       newNode.setLineno(leftChild.getLineno());
1040 |       newNode.setCharno(leftChild.getCharno());

1042 |       return newNode;

Original range: 107-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
107 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
108 |                                   String sourceString,
109 |                                   Config config,
110 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
111 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
112 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
113 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
114 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
115 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
116 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
117 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
118 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
119 | 
120 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
121 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
122 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
123 | 
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
147 |   }

/**
   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,
   * so make a best guess.

503 |   private static boolean mayBeStatementNoReturn(Node n) {
504 |     switch (n.getType()) {

507 |         // EMPTY and FUNCTION are used both in expression and statement
508 |         // contexts
509 |         return true;

513 |       case Token.CONST:
514 |       case Token.CONTINUE:

516 |       case Token.DO:
517 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
518 |       case Token.FOR:
519 |       case Token.IF:
520 |       case Token.LABEL:
521 |       case Token.SWITCH:
522 |       case Token.THROW:
523 |       case Token.TRY:
524 |       case Token.VAR:
525 |       case Token.WHILE:
526 |       case Token.WITH:
527 |         return true;
528 | 
529 |       default:
530 |         return false;
531 |     }

===== M0668 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3084-3112
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3084 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3085 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3086 |     if (info == null) {
3087 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3088 |       if (parent == null) {
3089 |         return null;
3090 |       }
3091 | 
3092 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3093 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3094 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3095 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3096 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
3097 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3098 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3099 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3100 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3101 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3102 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3103 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3104 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3105 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3106 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3107 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3108 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3109 |       }
3110 |     }
3111 |     return info;
3112 |   }

===== M0671 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silenceThreshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silenceThreshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silenceThreshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }
